# Incumbent switch checklists

These separate inventories record verified workflows in selected official sources. **Inventory coverage is incomplete for all three incumbents.** Other documented workflows may be omitted. Sampled pages, a sitemap, partner referrals and Terrace product boundaries cannot establish completeness.

Every inventoried workflow below remains a **Gap** until current passed replacement acceptance proves its actor, public door, prerequisites and outcome. A related DONE clause, generic CSV importer, staff-recorded substitute or boundary statement alone does not establish replacement. This page grants no migration, payment, provider or operational authority.

The [source-corpus manifest](switch-checklist-sources/manifest.json) retains separate inventories, captured-source hashes, source-to-workflow reconciliation and unaudited source areas. Source quotations are documentary claims by the incumbent; they establish no Terrace capability or provider entitlement.

## Corpus coverage and uncertainty

Public HTTPS response captured on the stated UTC date. Capture text decodes HTML entities, drops script, style, noscript and SVG contents, collapses whitespace by block, omits addresses and expands ambiguous word slash pairs. Raw response and retained normalized text have separate SHA-256 hashes. Hashes identify captures, not present-day source currency.

## AppFolio checklist

**Partial official-source inventory. 150 workflows; additional source areas remain unaudited.**

### AppFolio required named gaps

**Corporate accounting — Gap:** Company books and financial reporting remain unqualified replacements; residential accounting and product boundaries cannot remove this gap.

Documented in [Corporate accounting · af-accounting-01](#corporate-accounting-af-accounting-01) — [official source](https://www.appfolio.com/property-manager/accounting-reporting), captured 2026-11-05, section “Corporate Accounting”, line 43.

**Telephone maintenance intake — Gap:** Documented call intake remains a gap. Online repair forms, staff entry and provider referrals do not replace telephone maintenance intake.

Documented in [Telephone maintenance intake · af-smart-blog-01](#telephone-maintenance-intake-af-smart-blog-01) — [official source](https://www.appfolio.com/blog/smart-maintenance), captured 2026-11-05, section “So how does it work?”, line 21.

**Technician GPS — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.

**Scheduled reports — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.

#### Corporate accounting · af-accounting-01

**Gap:** Corporate books, reimbursements and a unified company chart are not replaced by residential subledgers.

**Related replacements:** Intentionally unmapped; no qualified Terrace replacement.

**Official source:** [af-accounting](https://www.appfolio.com/property-manager/accounting-reporting), captured 2026-11-05; section “Corporate Accounting”, [capture](switch-checklist-sources/af-accounting.txt) line 43.

> Bring your management company's books into the same platform as your properties, so nothing lives in a separate system or spreadsheet.

#### Automated bill coding and close · af-accounting-02

**Gap:** Same-capability replacement acceptance is unqualified for this documented workflow; inspect the current clause evidence and remaining gaps.

**Related replacements:** [ACCT-007](#clause-acct-007) (done); [ACCT-011](#clause-acct-011) (done); [AGENT-002](#clause-agent-002) (missing)

**Official source:** [af-accounting](https://www.appfolio.com/property-manager/accounting-reporting), captured 2026-11-05; section “Realm-X Accounting Performer”, [capture](switch-checklist-sources/af-accounting.txt) line 37.

> Nobody got into this business to spend their evenings entering bills. The Accounting Performer works alongside your accounting team to handle the most time-consuming tasks — coding bills, reconciling accounts, closing the books, and preparing budget forecasts — so your team focuses on the work that actually moves the business.

## Buildium checklist

**Partial official-source inventory. 55 workflows; additional source areas remain unaudited.**

### Buildium required named gaps

**Corporate accounting — Gap:** Company books and financial reporting remain unqualified replacements; residential accounting and product boundaries cannot remove this gap.

Documented in [Corporate accounting · bu-company-01](#corporate-accounting-bu-company-01) — [official source](https://www.buildium.com/features/company-financials/), captured 2026-11-05, section “Track the books for your business with the same accuracy and efficiency as your managed properties.”, line 72.

**Telephone maintenance intake — Gap:** Documented call intake remains a gap. Online repair forms, staff entry and provider referrals do not replace telephone maintenance intake.

Documented in [Telephone maintenance intake · bu-telephone-01](#telephone-maintenance-intake-bu-telephone-01) — [official source](https://www.buildium.com/maintenance-contact-center/), captured 2026-11-05, section “A trusted call center service to help you satisfy residents while reducing property risk”, line 69.

**Technician GPS — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.

**Scheduled reports — Gap:** Documented automatic report generation or delivery remains a gap; manual export does not qualify it.

Documented in [Scheduled reports · bu-automation-01](#scheduled-reports-bu-automation-01) — [official source](https://www.buildium.com/features/property-management-automation-software/), captured 2026-11-05, section “Reporting Automation”, line 78.
